expand=True, ) # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- # VUE : DOSSIER CONTRACTUEL (7 PAGES) - RESTAURATION COMPLÈTE MAKE & AUTOMATISATIONS # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- # LIEN STRIPE DEVIS : PAIEMENT STANDARD (AVEC TRANSMISSION DU DEVIS AU PAIEMENT) # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- # LIEN STRIPE DEVIS (CANONIQUE — SANS AUCUNE ERREUR 400) # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- def creer_lien_stripe_devis(devis_dict, ent_dict, page: ft.Page): """Génère le lien Stripe officiel pour le devis avec paramètres stricts.""" stripe_key = get_secret("STRIPE_SECRET_KEY", ent_dict.get("stripe_secret_key", "")).strip().strip("'\"") if not stripe_key: show_toast(page, "Clé Stripe introuvable dans Infisical !", is_error=True) return None num_devis = devis_dict.get("numero_devis", "DEV-OFFICIEL") client_nom = devis_dict.get("client_societe") or devis_dict.get("client_nom") or "Client" montant_ht = float(devis_dict.get("montant_setup") or 0.0) if montant_ht <= 0: show_toast(page, "Le montant du devis doit être supérieur à 0 €.", is_error=True) return None unit_amount_cents = int(round(montant_ht * 100)) nom_produit = f"Prestation d'Ingénierie — Devis {num_devis} ({client_nom})" try: price_payload = urllib.parse.urlencode({ "currency": "eur", "unit_amount": unit_amount_cents, "product_data[name]": nom_produit, "metadata[numero_devis]": num_devis, "metadata[client]": client_nom }).encode("utf-8") req_p = urllib.request.Request( "https://api.stripe.com/v1/prices", data=price_payload, headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {stripe_key}", "Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded"} ) ssl_ctx = ssl._create_unverified_context() with urllib.request.urlopen(req_p, context=ssl_ctx, timeout=12) as resp: price_id = json.loads(resp.read().decode("utf-8")).get("id") link_payload = urllib.parse.urlencode({ "line_items[0][price]": price_id, "line_items[0][quantity]": 1, "metadata[numero_devis]": num_devis, "metadata[client]": client_nom, "billing_address_collection": "required" }).encode("utf-8") req_l = urllib.request.Request( "https://api.stripe.com/v1/payment_links", data=link_payload, headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {stripe_key}", "Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded"} ) with urllib.request.urlopen(req_l, context=ssl_ctx, timeout=12) as resp_l: url = json.loads(resp_l.read().decode("utf-8")).get("url") if url: page.set_clipboard(url) show_toast(page, "💳 Lien Stripe copié !", is_error=False) return url except urllib.error.HTTPError as ex: err_detail = ex.read().decode("utf-8") print("❌ DÉTAIL ERREUR STRIPE :", err_detail) try: msg = json.loads(err_detail).get("error", {}).get("message", ex.reason) show_toast(page, f"Stripe : {msg}", is_error=True) except Exception: show_toast(page, f"Erreur Stripe {ex.code} : {ex.reason}", is_error=True) return None except Exception as ex: show_toast(page, f"Erreur système : {ex}", is_error=True) return None # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- # LIEN STRIPE 2 : FALLBACK SECOURS (STRIPE BILLING 4 MENSUALITÉS DIRECTES) # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- def creer_lien_stripe_billing_4x(devis_dict, ent_dict, page: ft.Page): # 🔐 Lecture sécurisée de la clé privée depuis Infisical stripe_key = get_secret("STRIPE_SECRET_KEY", ent_dict.get("stripe_secret_key", "")) if not stripe_key: show_toast(page, "Clé Stripe introuvable dans Infisical !", is_error=True) return None num_devis = devis_dict.get("numero_devis", "DEV-OFFICIEL") client_nom = devis_dict.get("client_societe") or devis_dict.get("client_nom") or "Client" montant_total = float(devis_dict.get("montant_setup") or 0.0) mensualite_cents = int(round((montant_total / 4.0) * 100)) try: # Tarif récurrent mensuel price_payload = urllib.parse.urlencode({ "currency": "eur", "unit_amount": mensualite_cents, "recurring[interval]": "month", "recurring[interval_count]": 1, "product_data[name]": f"Échéancier Garanti (1/4) — Devis {num_devis} ({client_nom})", "metadata[numero_devis]": num_devis, "metadata[client]": client_nom, "metadata[plan]": "FALLBACK_4X_SANS_ALMA" }).encode("utf-8") req_p = urllib.request.Request("https://api.stripe.com/v1/prices", data=price_payload, headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {stripe_key}", "Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded"}) ssl_ctx = ssl._create_unverified_context() with urllib.request.urlopen(req_p, context=ssl_ctx, timeout=12) as resp: price_id = json.loads(resp.read().decode("utf-8")).get("id") # Lien récurrent Stripe Billing link_payload = urllib.parse.urlencode({ "line_items[0][price]": price_id, "line_items[0][quantity]": 1, "metadata[numero_devis]": num_devis, "metadata[client]": client_nom, "metadata[echeances_total]": "4", "billing_address_collection": "required" }).encode("utf-8") req_l = urllib.request.Request("https://api.stripe.com/v1/payment_links", data=link_payload, headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {stripe_key}", "Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded"}) with urllib.request.urlopen(req_l, context=ssl_ctx, timeout=12) as resp_l: fallback_url = json.loads(resp_l.read().decode("utf-8")).get("url") if fallback_url: page.set_clipboard(fallback_url) show_toast(page, f"🔄 Lien Secours Garanti (4x {(montant_total/4.0):.2f} €) copié !") return fallback_url except Exception as ex: show_toast(page, f"Erreur Stripe Billing : {ex}", is_error=True) return None def declencher_whatsapp_parcours_vip(numero_devis: str, page: ft.Page = None) -> bool: """ Envoie le lien officiel vers la vitrine index.html pour signature du Mandat Jalon 1 (500 €) AVANT toute signature du devis Themis (conformité stricte Art. 6 RGEC FEDER). """ ent = get_entreprise_info() webhook_url = get_secret("WEBHOOK_MAKE_WHATSAPP", "").strip() or ent.get("webhook_make_relance", "").strip() if not webhook_url: print("⚠️ [WHATSAPP VIP] Aucun Webhook Make configuré.") return False conn = get_db() conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row cursor = conn.cursor() cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM devis WHERE numero_devis = ?", (numero_devis,)) d = cursor.fetchone() conn.close() if not d or not d["client_telephone"]: return False prenom = d["client_prenom"] or "Cher Dirigeant" ste_client = d["client_societe"] or d["client_nom"] or "votre projet" siret = d["client_siret"] or "" tel_clean = formater_telephone_international(d["client_telephone"]) # 🔗 Construction de l'URL vers la vitrine index.html avec déclenchement de la modale Mandat params_vitrine = { "action": "mandat", "devis": numero_devis, "ste": ste_client, "rep": prenom, "siret": siret, "tel": tel_clean, "email": d.get("client_email") or "" # ✅ Garanti présent } lien_mandat_vitrine = f"https://dhamusoft.fr/?{urllib.parse.urlencode(params_vitrine)}" payload_vip = { "event": "envoi_instruction_mandat_feder", "type_evenement": "MANDAT_JALON1_PREALABLE", "numero_devis_proforma": numero_devis, "destinataire_telephone": tel_clean, "client_email": d["client_email"], "var_1_prenom": prenom, "var_2_projet": ste_client, "lien_mandat_vitrine": lien_mandat_vitrine, "montant_jalon_1": "500,00 € HT", "reste_charge_jalon_1": "100,00 € HT" } try: req = urllib.request.Request( webhook_url, data=json.dumps(payload_vip).encode("utf-8"), headers={"Content-Type": "application/json", "User-Agent": "Themis-FEDER/1.0"} ) ssl_ctx = ssl._create_unverified_context() with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=10, context=ssl_ctx) as resp: print(f"🚀 [WHATSAPP FEDER] Invitation Mandat Jalon 1 transmise pour {prenom} ({tel_clean})") if page: show_toast(page, f"📲 Lien Mandat Jalon 1 expédié à {prenom} !") return True except Exception as ex: print(f"❌ [WHATSAPP FEDER ERREUR] : {ex}") return False # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- # VUE : DOSSIER CONTRACTUEL (7 PAGES) & GESTION DES SIGNATURES # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- def view_dossier_contractuel(page: ft.Page): conn = get_db() items = pd.read_sql_query( """ SELECT numero_devis, client_societe, client_nom, client_prenom, client_email, statut, contrat_signe, date_signature_contrat, client_id, signature_b64 FROM devis WHERE archive = 0 ORDER BY id DESC """, conn ) ent = get_entreprise_info() conn.close() default_devis = str(items.iloc[0]["numero_devis"]) if not items.empty else None dd_select = ft.Dropdown( label="Sélectionner le dossier de devis :", options=[ ft.dropdown.Option( key=str(row["numero_devis"]), text=f"{row['numero_devis']} — {row['client_societe'] or row['client_nom']} ({'Signé ✅' if row['contrat_signe'] == 1 else 'Non signé ⚠️'})" ) for _, row in items.iterrows() ], value=default_devis, width=380, border_color="#CBD5E1", ) signature_status_box = ft.Container(visible=False) txt_date_sign = ft.TextField(label="Date de signature effective (AAAA-MM-JJ)", value=str(datetime.date.today()), width=240) selected_file_path = None lbl_file_selected = ft.Text("Aucun fichier de scan sélectionné.", size=12, italic=True, color="#64748B") def on_file_picked(e: ft.FilePickerResultEvent): nonlocal selected_file_path if e.files and len(e.files) > 0: selected_file_path = e.files[0].path lbl_file_selected.value = f"📄 Prêt à archiver : {os.path.basename(selected_file_path)}" lbl_file_selected.color = "#047857" page.update() file_picker = ft.FilePicker(on_result=on_file_picked) page.overlay.append(file_picker) txt_scan_note = ft.TextField(label="Description ou référence du scan (ex: Bon de commande signé)", expand=True) scans_history_container = ft.Container() def refresh_scans_history(devis_num): conn_sc = get_db() df_scans = pd.read_sql_query("SELECT * FROM documents_clients WHERE devis_id = ? ORDER BY id DESC", conn_sc, params=(devis_num,)) conn_sc.close() if df_scans.empty: scans_history_container.content = ft.Text("Aucun document scanné n'a encore été rattaché à ce devis.", italic=True, heading_row_color="#F8FAFC", border=ft.border.all(1, "#CBD5E1"), border_radius=8, ) page.update() def on_devis_change(e): if not dd_select.value: signature_status_box.visible = False page.update() return conn_c = get_db() cursor_c = conn_c.cursor() cursor_c.execute("SELECT statut, contrat_signe, date_signature_contrat, client_societe, client_nom FROM devis WHERE numero_devis = ?", (dd_select.value,)) d = cursor_c.fetchone() conn_c.close() if not d: signature_status_box.visible = False page.update() return is_signe = (d[1] == 1) statut_devis = d[0] signature_status_box.content = ft.Icon(ft.icons.CHECK_CIRCLE_ROUNDED if is_signe else ft.icons.WARNING_AMBER_ROUNDED, color="#047857" if is_signe else "#B91C1C", size=22), ft.Column([ ft.Text( f"Statut Devis : {statut_devis} | Dossier Contractuel : {'VALIDÉ & SIGNÉ' if is_signe else 'NON SIGNÉ (Émission de factures bloquée)'}", weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD, color="#065F46" if is_signe else "#7F1D1D", size=13 ), ft.Text( f"Date de signature enregistrée : {d[2] or 'Aucune'}" if is_signe else "Pour débloquer la facturation, faites signer le devis en ligne ou validez-le manuellement ci-dessous.", size=11, color="#047857" if ------------------------------------------------------------------------- # AUTOMATISATION 1 : DISPATCH MAKE DU DEVIS COMPLET POUR SIGNATURE EMAIL # ------------------------------------------------------------------------- def dispatcher_devis_signature_make(e): if not dd_select.value: show_toast(page, "Sélectionnez un devis d'abord.", is_error=True) return conn_m = get_db() conn_m.row_factory = sqlite3.Row c = conn_m.cursor() c.execute("SELECT * FROM devis WHERE numero_devis = ?", (dd_select.value,)) d = dict(c.fetchone()) ent_info = get_entreprise_info() conn_m.close() if not d.get("client_email"): show_toast(page, "Aucune adresse e-mail enregistrée pour ce client sur son devis.", is_error=True) return webhook_url = get_webhook_make("EMAIL") if not webhook_url: show_toast(page, "Configurez l'URL Webhook Make dans 'Paramètres & Fiscalité' !", is_error=True) return # 🎯 DÉTECTION DU PARCOURS : FEDER VS DIRECT SANS SUBVENTION type_parcours = d.get("type_parcours") or "FEDER_MANDAT" is_direct = (type_parcours == "DIRECT_STANDARD") montant_total_ht = float(d.get("montant_setup") or 0.0) pct_acompte = int(d.get("pourcentage_acompte") or 30) montant_acompte_ht = round(montant_total_ht * (pct_acompte / 100.0), 2) montant_solde_ht = round(montant_total_ht - montant_acompte_ht, 2) ste_client = d.get("client_societe") or d.get("client_nom") nom_complet = f"{d.get('client_prenom', '')} {d.get('client_nom', '')}".strip() dossier_html_officiel = generate_dossier_contractuel_html(d, ent_info, signature_b64=d.get("signature_b64")) # 🎯 PAYLOAD COMPLET & AIGUILLÉ (TOUS LES ALIAS INCLUS) payload_enrichi = { # Aiguillage Make : Ligne E si Direct, Ligne D si FEDER "event": "envoi_devis_definitif_signature" if is_direct else "envoi_devis_proforma", "type_evenement": "COMMANDE_DIRECTE_FERME" if is_direct else "DEVIS_PROFORMA_AVANT_AR", "type_parcours": type_parcours, # 📊 MONTANTS AVEC TOUTES LES CASSES (PLUS AUCUN BLANC DANS L'EMAIL) : "Montant_HT": f"{montant_total_ht:.2f}", "montant_ht": f"{montant_total_ht:.2f}", "Acompte_Pct": pct_acompte, "pourcentage_acompte": pct_acompte, "montant_acompte_ht": f"{montant_acompte_ht:.2f}", "montant_solde_ht": f"{montant_solde_ht:.2f}", # 📋 COORDONNÉES CLIENT & DEVIS (ALIGNÉS SUR GOOGLE SHEETS) : "Numero_Devis": ''}&tel={d.get('client_telephone') or ''}&email={urllib.parse.quote(d['client_email'] or '')}", "lien_signature_complet": f"https://dhamusoft.fr/signer_contrat.html?devis={d['numero_devis']}", "corps_dossier_7p_html": dossier_html_officiel } try: ssl_context = ssl._create_unverified_context() req = urllib.request.Request( webhook_url, data=json.dumps(payload_enrichi).encode("utf-8"), """, (txt_date_sign.value.strip(), dd_select.value), ) conn_u.commit() conn_u.close() # 🚀 Déclencheur unifié anti-court-circuit finaliser_signature_et_declencher_flux(dd_select.value, page) on_devis_change(None) def attach_scanned_file(e): nonlocal selected_file_path if not dd_select.value or not selected_file_path: show_toast(page, "Sélectionnez un devis et choisissez un scan d'abord.", is_error=True) return conn_att = get_db() cursor_att = conn_att.cursor() cursor_att.execute("SELECT client_id FROM devis WHERE numero_devis = ?", (dd_select.value,)) cid_res = cursor_att.fetchone() cid = cid_res[0] if cid_res else 1 fname = f"{dd_select.value}_scan_{datetime.date.today().strftime('%Y%m%d%H%M%S')}_{os.path.basename(selected_file_path)}" dest_path = os.path.join(SCANS_DIR, fname) shutil.copyfile(selected_file_path, dest_path) cursor_att.execute( """ INSERT INTO documents_clients (client_id, devis_id, nom_document, chemin_fichier, date_upload, notes) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?) --------------------------------------------------------------------------- # VUE : PARAMÈTRES, FISCALITÉ & SIGNATURE PRESTATAIRE UNIQUE # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- def view_settings(page: ft.Page): ent = get_entreprise_info() # Cotisations & Impôts txt_taux_urssaf = ft.TextField(label="Cotisations URSSAF (%)", value=f"{ent['taux_urssaf']:.1f}", width=220, border_color="#CBD5E1") txt_taux_ir = ft.TextField(label="Prélèvement Libératoire IR (%)", value=f"{ent['taux_ir']:.1f}", width=220, border_color="#CBD5E1") # 3. Guadeloupe, Martinique, Réunion)"), ft.dropdown.Option("20.0", "20,0 % (France Métropolitaine)"), ], value=f"{ent.get('taux_tva_defaut'):.1f}" if f"{ent.get('taux_tva_defaut'):.1f}" in ["8.5", "20.0"] else "8.5", width=380, border_color="#CBD5E1", ) txt_tva_intercom = ft.TextField( label="Numéro de TVA Intracommunautaire", value=ent.get("numero_tva_intercom", ""), width=300, border_color="#CBD5E1", ) def on_tva_toggle(e): dd_taux_tva.disabled = not chk_assujetti_tva.value txt_tva_intercom.disabled = not chk_assujetti_tva.value page.update() chk_assujetti_tva.on_change = on_tva_toggle dd_taux_tva.disabled = not chk_assujetti_tva.value txt_tva_intercom.disabled = not chk_assujetti_tva.value # ------------------------------------------------------------------------- # BLOC SIGNATURE OFFICIELLE DU PRESTATAIRE (STOCKAGE UNIQUE) # ------------------------------------------------------------------------- sig_actuelle_b64 = ent.get("signature_prestataire_b64", "") img_preview_sig = ft.Image( src_base64=sig_actuelle_b64 if sig_actuelle_b64 else None, width=220, height=80, fit=ft.ImageFit.CONTAIN, visible=bool(sig_actuelle_b64) ) lbl_sig_status = ft.Text( "Signature active et scellée sur tous vos documents ✅" if sig_actuelle_b64 else "Aucune signature enregistrée ⚠️ (Vos documents sortiront non signés par le prestataire)", size=11, color="#047857" if sig_actuelle_b64 else "#B45309", weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD ) def on_sig_file_picked(ev: ft.FilePickerResultEvent): if ev.files and len(ev.files) > 0: fichier_path = ev.files[0].path try: with open(fichier_path, "rb") as f_img: raw_b64 = base64.b64encode(f_img.read()).decode("utf-8") # Mise à jour immédiate en base SQLite conn_sig = get_db() conn_sig.cursor().execute("UPDATE entreprise SET signature_prestataire_b64 = ? WHERE id = 1", (raw_b64,)) conn_sig.commit() conn_sig.close() img_preview_sig.src_base64 = raw_b64 img_preview_sig.visible = True lbl_sig_status.value = "Nouvelle signature enregistrée et active sur 100% des documents ✅" lbl_sig_status.color = "#047857" page.update() show_toast(page, "Signature officielle enregistrée avec succès !") except Exception as ex: show_toast(page, f"Erreur de lecture de l'image : {ex}", is_error=True) def supprimer_signature_prestataire(e): conn_del = get_db() conn_del.cursor().execute("UPDATE entreprise SET signature_prestataire_b64 = '' WHERE id = 1") conn_del.commit() conn_del.close() img_preview_sig.visible = False lbl_sig_status.value = "Signature retirée. Vos documents sortiront sans signature prestataire." lbl_sig_status.color = "#B45309" page.update() show_toast(page, "Signature prestataire supprimée.") # ------------------------------------------------------------------------- # BLOC SIGNATURE PRESTATAIRE AVEC PAD TACTILE (ZÉRO PROBLÈME DE FICHIER) # ------------------------------------------------------------------------- sig_actuelle_b64 = ent.get("signature_prestataire_b64", "") img_preview_sig = ft.Image( src_base64=sig_actuelle_b64 if sig_actuelle_b64 else None, width=220, height=75, fit=ft.ImageFit.CONTAIN, visible=bool(sig_actuelle_b64) ) lbl_sig_status = ft.Text( "Signature active et scellée sur tous vos documents ✅" if sig_actuelle_b64 else "Aucune signature enregistrée ⚠️ (Vos documents sortiront non signés)", size=11, color="#047857" if sig_actuelle_b64 else "#B45309", weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD ) def ouvrir_pad_signature(e): # Ouvre la page de signature tactile servie par Themis webbrowser.open(f"http://localhost:{PASSERELLE_PORT}/signer-prestataire") show_toast(page, "Pad de signature ouvert dans votre navigateur !") def rafraichir_apercu_signature(e): # Rafraîchit l'aperçu après signature dans le navigateur ent_fraiche = get_entreprise_info() nouvelle_sig = ent_fraiche.get("signature_prestataire_b64", "") if nouvelle_sig: img_preview_sig.src_base64 = nouvelle_sig img_preview_sig.visible = True lbl_sig_status.value = "Signature active et scellée sur tous vos documents ✅" lbl_sig_status.color = "#047857" page.update() show_toast(page, "Signature actualisée et synchronisée avec succès !") else: show_toast(page, "Aucune signature détectée. WHERE id=1 """, ( txt_nom.value.strip(), txt_adresse.value.strip(), txt_siret.value.strip(), txt_rcs.value.strip(), txt_forme.value.strip(), t_u, t_i, txt_iban.value.strip().replace(" ", "").upper(), txt_bic.value.strip().upper(), txt_banque.value.strip(), txt_titulaire.value.strip(), txt_webhook_make.value.strip(), 1 if chk_assujetti_tva.value else 0, t_tva, txt_tva_intercom.value.strip().upper() ) ) conn.commit() conn.close() show_toast(page, "Paramètres d'entreprise enregistrés !") page.go("/dashboard") def print_attestation_art286(e): html = generate_attestation_conformite_html(ent) with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile("w", delete=False, suffix=".html", encoding="utf-8") as tf: tf.write(html) temp_path = tf.name webbrowser.open(f"file://{temp_path}") show_toast(page, "Attestation de Conformité Art. 286 du CGI générée.") def do_system_backup(e): today_str = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S") backups_dir = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, "backups_systeme") os.makedirs(backups_dir, exist_ok=True) zip_name = f"themis_backup_{today_str}.zip" zip_path = os.path.join(backups_dir, zip_name) try: with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_path, "w", zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zipf: if os.path.exists(DB_PATH): zipf.write(DB_PATH, arcname="devis_suivi.db") if os.path.exists(SCANS_DIR): for root, _, files in os.walk(SCANS_DIR): for file in files: abs_file = os.path.join(root, file) rel_file = os.path.relpath(abs_file, BASE_DIR) zipf.write(abs_file, arcname=rel_file) show_toast(page, f"Sauvegarde réussie : {zip_name}") webbrowser.open(f"file://{backups_dir}") except Exception as ex: show_toast(page, f"Erreur de sauvegarde : {str(ex)}", is_error=True) return ft.ListView( controls=[ create_header("SET.01", "PARAMÈTRES & FISCALITÉ", "CONFIGURATION OFFICIELLE • COORDONNÉES BANCAIRES & TVA"), # Carte 1 : Paramètres Généraux, Bancaires et Fiscaux create_card( ft.Column(controls=[ ft.Text("1. --------------------------------------------------------------------------- # SERVEUR WEBHOOK D'ÉCOUTE & PASSERELLE AUTOMATIQUE # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- PASSERELLE_SECRET_TOKEN = get_secret("PASSERELLE_SECRET_TOKEN", "THEMIS_SECURE_MANDAT_2026_DOM") PASSERELLE_PORT = int(os.environ.get("PORT", 8765)) # =========================================================================== # PHASE A : MOTEURS D'AUTOMATISATION DU CYCLE FINANCIER (ZÉRO CLIC) # =========================================================================== # =========================================================================== # ENGIN POST-SIGNATURE : ACOMPTE LOCAL + DISPATCHEUR ÉTANCHE MAKE # =========================================================================== def emettre_acompte_automatique(numero_devis: str, page: ft.Page = None) -> str: """Émet et scelle l'acompte 1/4 localement dans SQLite sans appeler Make directement.""" conn = get_db() conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row cursor = conn.cursor() cursor.execute( "SELECT numero_facture FROM factures WHERE numero_devis = ? AND (type_facture LIKE '%Acompte%' OR type_facture LIKE '%Jalon 1%')", (numero_devis,) ) existant = cursor.fetchone() if existant: conn.close() return existant[0] today_str = datetime.date.today().strftime("%Y-%m-%d") today_compact = datetime.date.today().strftime("%Y%m") if str(numero_devis).startswith("TIC-"): siret = numero_devis.replace("TIC-", "") cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM dossiers_cheque_tic WHERE siret = ?", (siret,)) tic = cursor.fetchone() if not tic: conn.close() return "" ste_tag = sanitize_tag(tic["nom_entreprise"]) num_facture = f"FAC-A_J1_{today_compact}_{ste_tag}" montant_ht = 500.00 # ✅ 500 € conforme FEDER type_facture = "Facture Jalon 1 — Cadrage & Dépôt (500 € HT)" else: cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM devis WHERE numero_devis = ?", (numero_devis,)) d = cursor.fetchone() if not d: conn.close() return "" setup = float(d["montant_setup"] or 0.0) if setup <= 0: conn.close() return "" nom_client = d["client_societe"] or f"{d['client_prenom']} {d['client_nom']}".strip() client_tag = sanitize_tag(nom_client) num_facture = f"FAC-A_{numero_devis}_{client_tag}" montant_ht = round(setup / 4.0, 2) type_facture = "Facture d'Acompte (1/4 — Démarrage)" cursor.execute("SELECT hash_sha256 FROM factures WHERE hash_sha256 IS NOT NULL ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1") last_h = cursor.fetchone() prev_hash = last_h[0] if last_h and last_h[0] else "THEMIS-GENESIS-SEAL" empreinte = f"{prev_hash}|{num_facture}|{numero_devis}|{today_str}|{montant_ht:.2f}" curr_hash = hashlib.sha256(empreinte.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest() echeance_str = (datetime.date.today() + datetime.timedelta(days=15)).strftime("%Y-%m-%d") cursor.execute( """ INSERT INTO factures (numero_facture, numero_devis, type_facture, date_facture, date_echeance, montant_ht, statut, prev_hash, hash_sha256) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'Facture Émise', ?, ?) """, (num_facture, numero_devis, type_facture, today_str, echeance_str, montant_ht, prev_hash, curr_hash) ) conn.commit() conn.close() print(f"✅ [THEMIS DB] Facture d'acompte {num_facture} ({montant_ht:.2f} €) scellée.") return num_facture def finaliser_signature_et_declencher_flux(numero_devis: str, page: ft.Page = None): """ Orchestrateur unique : 1. Crée l'acompte en base. 2. Envoie l'e-mail officiel à WEBHOOK_MAKE_EMAIL. 3. Envoie le WhatsApp VIP à WEBHOOK_MAKE_WHATSAPP. """ conn = get_db() conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row cursor = conn.cursor() cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM devis WHERE numero_devis = ?", (numero_devis,)) d = cursor.fetchone() conn.close() if not d: return # 1. Émission locale de l'acompte num_fac = emettre_acompte_automatique(numero_devis, page) # 2. ENVOI E-MAIL VIA INFISICAL (WEBHOOK_MAKE_EMAIL) url_email = get_webhook_make("EMAIL") if url_email and d["client_email"]: try: ent_info = get_entreprise_info() html_contrat = contract_templates.generate_dossier_contractuel_html( d=d, ent=ent_info, signature_b64=d["signature_b64"] ) payload_email = { "event": "contrat_signe_definitif", "numero_devis": numero_devis, "numero_facture_acompte": num_fac, "destinataire_email": d["client_email"], "client_nom": f"{d['client_prenom']} {d['client_nom']}".strip(), "client_societe": d["client_societe"] or "", "client_telephone": d["client_telephone"] or "", "corps_dossier_7p_html": html_contrat } req_e = urllib.request.Request( url_email, data=json.dumps(payload_email).encode("utf-8"), headers={"Content-Type": "application/json", "User-Agent": "Themis-Email/1.0"} ) ssl_ctx = ssl._create_unverified_context() with urllib.request.urlopen(req_e, timeout=10, context=ssl_ctx) as resp: print(f"📧 [MAKE EMAIL] Contrat 7P & Acompte transmis à Make pour {d['client_email']}") except Exception as ex: print(f"❌ [MAKE EMAIL ERREUR] : {ex}") time.sleep(0.4) # Pause tampon anti-collision # 3. ENVOI WHATSAPP VIP VIA INFISICAL (WEBHOOK_MAKE_WHATSAPP) url_whatsapp = get_webhook_make("WHATSAPP") if url_whatsapp and d["client_telephone"]: try: prenom = d["client_prenom"] or "Cher Partenaire" ste_client = d["client_societe"] or d["client_nom"] or "votre projet" montant_ht = f"{float(d['montant_setup'] or 0):,.2f} € HT" tel_clean = formater_telephone_international(d["client_telephone"]) # ✅ Domaine officiel dhamusoft.fr avec modale mandat params = { "action": "mandat", "devis": numero_devis, "siret": d["client_siret"] or "", "ste": ste_client, "rep": prenom, "tel": tel_clean } lien_mandat = f"https://dhamusoft.fr/?{urllib.parse.urlencode(params)}" payload_wa = { "event": "declencher_parcours_vip_whatsapp", "type_evenement": "WHATSAPP_VIP_CHEQUE_TIC", "numero_devis": numero_devis, "destinataire_telephone": tel_clean, "var_1_prenom": prenom, "var_2_projet": ste_client, "var_3_montant": montant_ht, "lien_mandat_vitrine": lien_mandat } req_w = urllib.request.Request( url_whatsapp, data=json.dumps(payload_wa).encode("utf-8"), headers={"Content-Type": "application/json", "User-Agent": "Themis-WA/1.0"} ) ssl_ctx = ssl._create_unverified_context() with urllib.request.urlopen(req_w, timeout=10, context=ssl_ctx) as resp: print(f"📲 [MAKE WHATSAPP] Message VIP transmis pour {tel_clean}") if page: show_toast(page, f"✅ Contrat validé : E-mail et WhatsApp expédiés !") except Exception as ex: print(f"❌ [MAKE WHATSAPP ERREUR] : {ex}") def traiter_encaissement_automatique(numero_facture: str, montant_percu: float, ref_reglement: str, mode_paiement: str = "Carte bancaire (Stripe)", commission_estimee: float = None) -> bool: """ Lettrage comptable Zéro Clic : 1. Bascule la facture en 'Facture Acquittée'. 2. Inscrit le montant BRUT dans le Livre des Recettes (URSSAF). 3. Inscrit les commissions bancaires dans le Registre des Dépenses (Frais Bancaires). """ conn = get_db() conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row cursor = conn.cursor() cursor.execute("SELECT * FROM factures WHERE numero_facture = ?", (numero_facture,)) f_row = cursor.fetchone() if not f_row: conn.close() print(f"❌ [LETTRAGE] Facture {numero_facture} introuvable en base.") return False # Éviter un double lettrage if f_row["statut"] in ["Facture Acquittée", "Payée"]: conn.close() print(f"ℹ️ [LETTRAGE] Facture {numero_facture} déjà acquittée.") return True cursor.execute("UPDATE factures SET statut = 'Facture Acquittée' WHERE numero_facture = ?", (numero_facture,)) # Récupération des informations client rattachées client_nom = "Client Direct" cursor.execute("SELECT client_societe, client_nom, client_prenom FROM devis WHERE numero_devis = ?", (f_row["numero_devis"],)) d_row = cursor.fetchone() if d_row: client_nom = d_row["client_societe"] or f"{d_row['client_prenom']} {d_row['client_nom']}" today_str = datetime.date.today().strftime("%Y-%m-%d") # Inscription au Livre des Recettes (URSSAF : Sur encaissement brut) cursor.execute( """ INSERT INTO paiements (numero_facture, date_paiement, client_nom, nature_prestation, montant_recu, mode_paiement, reference_reglement) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?) """, (numero_facture, today_str, client_nom, f_row["type_facture"], montant_percu, mode_paiement, ref_reglement) ) # Inscription de la commission au Registre des Dépenses # Barème standard Stripe Europe (1,5% + 0,25 €) si non spécifiée if commission_estimee is None or commission_estimee <= 0: if "Stripe" in mode_paiement: commission_estimee = round((montant_percu * 0.015) + 0.25, 2) elif "PayPal" in mode_paiement: commission_estimee = round((montant_percu * 0.029) + 0.35, 2) else: commission_estimee = 0.0 if commission_estimee > 0: cursor.execute( """ INSERT INTO depenses (date_depense, fournisseur, categorie, montant, mode_paiement, justificatif_ref, notes) VALUES (?, ?, 'Frais Bancaires / Assurances', ?, 'Prélèvement', ?, ?) """, ( today_str, "Stripe Payments Europe" if "Stripe" in mode_paiement else "PayPal Europe", commission_estimee, ref_reglement, f"Frais de passerelle prélevés sur {numero_facture}" ) ) conn.commit() conn.close() print(f"🎉 [LETTRAGE SUCCÈS] Facture {numero_facture} acquittée ! Recette : {montant_percu:.2f} € | Commission : {commission_estimee:.2f} €") return True class MandatWebhookHandler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler): def do_GET(self): # ✍️ PAGE DU PAD DE SIGNATURE PRESTATAIRE if self.path == "/signer-prestataire": html_pad = f""" Pad de Signature Officielle • Dhamusoft

✍️ Signature Officielle du Prestataire

Tracez votre signature au doigt ou à la souris. Elle sera enregistrée une seule fois et apposée sur 100% de vos contrats 7P, devis et factures.

ZONE DE SIGNATURE MANUSCRITE En attente de tracé...
""" self.send_response(200) self.send_header("Content-Type", "text/html; charset=utf-8") self.end_headers() self.wfile.write(html_pad.encode("utf-8")) return self.send_response(404) self.end_headers() def do_POST(self): content_length = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length", 0)) body = self.rfile.read(content_length) # --------------------------------------------------------------------- # ROUTE 1 : ENREGISTREMENT SIGNATURE PRESTATAIRE (PAD WEB) # --------------------------------------------------------------------- if self.path == "/api/enregistrer-signature-prestataire": try: data = json.loads(body.decode("utf-8")) sig_b64 = data.get("signature_b64", "") if sig_b64: conn = get_db() conn.cursor().execute("UPDATE entreprise SET signature_prestataire_b64 = ? WHERE id = 1", (sig_b64,)) conn.commit() conn.close() self.send_response(200) self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json") self.end_headers() self.wfile.write(json.dumps({"success": True}).encode("utf-8")) return except Exception as e: self.send_response(500) self.end_headers() return # --------------------------------------------------------------------- # ROUTE 2 : WEBHOOK D'ENCAISSEMENT EN TEMPS RÉEL (STRIPE / PAYPAL / MAKE) # --------------------------------------------------------------------- if self.path in ["/webhook/stripe", "/webhook/paiement"]: try: data = json.loads(body.decode("utf-8")) f_num = "" montant = 0.0 ref_trans = f"STRIPE_{int(time.time())}" mode = "Carte bancaire (Stripe)" # Cas 1 : Payload natif Stripe if "type" in data and "data" in data: event_type = data.get("type", "") obj = data["data"].get("object", {}) if event_type in ["checkout.session.completed", "payment_intent.succeeded", "invoice.paid"]: metadata = obj.get("metadata", {}) f_num = metadata.get("numero_facture", "") or obj.get("description", "") if not f_num and "Facture " in obj.get("description", ""): f_num = obj.get("description", "").split("Facture ")[-1].split(" ")[0].strip() raw_cents = obj.get("amount_total") or obj.get("amount") or 0 montant = float(raw_cents) / 100.0 ref_trans = obj.get("id") or ref_trans mode = "Carte bancaire (Stripe Billing)" if event_type == "invoice.paid" else "Carte bancaire (Stripe)" # Cas 2 : Payload universel Themis / Make / PayPal else: f_num = data.get("numero_facture") or data.get("facture") or "" montant = float(data.get("montant_recu") or data.get("montant") or 0.0) ref_trans = data.get("reference") or data.get("transaction_id") or ref_trans mode = data.get("mode") or data.get("mode_paiement") or "Carte bancaire" if f_num and montant > 0: ok = traiter_encaissement_automatique(f_num, montant, ref_trans, mode) self.send_response(200 if ok else 400) self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json") self.end_headers() self.wfile.write(json.dumps({"success": ok, "facture": f_num, "montant": montant}).encode("utf-8")) return else: self.send_response(422) self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json") self.end_headers() self.wfile.write(json.dumps({"error": "Numéro de facture ou montant manquant"}).encode("utf-8")) return except Exception as e: self.send_response(500) self.end_headers() self.wfile.write(json.dumps({"error": str(e)}).encode("utf-8")) return # --------------------------------------------------------------------- # ROUTE 3 : PARSING DES ÉVÉNEMENTS POST-SIGNATURE & PROSPECTS # --------------------------------------------------------------------- try: data = json.loads(body.decode("utf-8")) # 🎯 3.A : DÉTECTION DU MANDAT JALON 1 SIGNÉ DEPUIS INDEX.HTML (CANVAS) if data.get("event") == "mandat_jalon1_signe_web" or data.get("type_evenement") == "MANDAT_JALON1_SIGNE": siret = re.sub(r"\s+", "", str(data.get("siret", "")).strip()) devis_ref = str(data.get("devis_themis_ref", "")).strip() sig_png = str(data.get("signature_png", "")).strip() date_sig = str(datetime.date.today()) conn = get_db() cursor = conn.cursor() cursor.execute(""" UPDATE dossiers_cheque_tic SET statut_dossier = 'Mandat Jalon 1 Signé', statut_pipeline = 'MANDAT_JALON1_SIGNE', date_mandat = ?, signature_png = ?, jalon_1_montant_ht = 500.0, jalon_1_reste_charge_ht = 100.0, jalon_2_montant_ht = 500.0, jalon_2_reste_charge_ht = 100.0, total_mission_ht = 1000.0 WHERE siret = ? OR devis_origine = ? """, (date_sig, sig_png, siret, devis_ref)) # Scellement SHA-256 de la Facture Jalon 1 (500 € HT) num_fac_j1 = f"FAC-J1_{siret[:8]}_{datetime.date.today().strftime('%Y%m')}" cursor.execute("SELECT hash_sha256 FROM factures WHERE hash_sha256 IS NOT NULL ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1") last_h = cursor.fetchone() prev_h = last_h[0] if last_h and last_h[0] else "THEMIS-GENESIS-SEAL" curr_h = hashlib.sha256(f"{prev_h}|{num_fac_j1}|{date_sig}|500.00".encode("utf-8")).hexdigest() cursor.execute(""" INSERT OR IGNORE INTO factures (numero_facture, numero_devis, type_facture, date_facture, date_echeance, montant_ht, statut, prev_hash, hash_sha256) VALUES (?, ?, 'Facture Jalon 1 — Cadrage & Dépôt (500 € HT)', ?, ?, 500.0, 'Facture Émise', ?, ?) """, (num_fac_j1, devis_ref or f"TIC-{siret}", date_sig, date_sig, prev_h, curr_h)) conn.commit() conn.close() print(f"✅ [FEDER PIPELINE] Mandat Jalon 1 validé pour {siret} — Facture Jalon 1 ({num_fac_j1}) émise.") self.send_response(200) self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json") self.end_headers() self.wfile.write(json.dumps({"success": True, "message": f"Mandat Jalon 1 scellé pour {siret}", "facture": num_fac_j1}).encode("utf-8")) return # 🎯 3.B : SIGNATURE CONTRAT 7P EN LIGNE if data.get("event") == "contrat_7p_signe_en_ligne" or data.get("type_evenement") == "CONTRAT_7P_SIGNE": num_devis = data.get("numero_devis") date_sig = data.get("date_signature") or str(datetime.date.today()) sig_png = data.get("Signature_PNG") or data.get("signature_png") or "" ip_sig = data.get("ip_signataire") or "127.0.0.1" conn = get_db() cursor = conn.cursor() cursor.execute( """ UPDATE devis SET statut = 'Validé & Signé', contrat_signe = 1, date_signature_contrat = ?, signature_b64 = ? WHERE numero_devis = ? """, (date_sig, sig_png, num_devis) ) try: cursor.execute( """ INSERT INTO documents_clients (client_id, devis_id, nom_document, chemin_fichier, date_upload, notes) VALUES (1, ?, 'Signature_Manuscrite_7P_Scellee.png', ?, ?, ?) """, (num_devis, sig_png[:200] if sig_png else "SIG_PNG_OK", date_sig, f"Signé en ligne le {date_sig} (IP: {ip_sig})") ) except Exception: pass conn.commit() conn.close() # Déclenchement du dispatcheur étanche (Acompte + Email + WhatsApp) finaliser_signature_et_declencher_flux(num_devis) self.send_response(200) self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json") self.end_headers() self.wfile.write(json.dumps({"success": True, "message": f"Contrat 7P validé pour {num_devis}"}).encode("utf-8")) return # 🎯 3.C : INTÉGRATION INITIALE PROSPECTS DU SITE WEB (CHÈQUE TIC) nom_entreprise = data.get("entreprise") or data.get("nom_entreprise", "").strip() siret = re.sub(r"\s+", "", str(data.get("siret", "")).strip()) commune = data.get("commune", "Baie-Mahault").strip() representant = data.get("representant_legal", "").strip() email = (data.get("email") or data.get("destinataire_email") or data.get("Email") or "").strip() tel = data.get("telephone_client", "").strip() if not nom_entreprise or not siret or not representant: self.send_response(400) self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json") self.end_headers() self.wfile.write(json.dumps({"error": "Champs obligatoires manquants"}).encode("utf-8")) return b_web = float(data.get("budget_web_ht", 3200.0)) b_secu = float(data.get("budget_securite_ht", 1800.0)) b_themis = float(data.get("budget_themis_logiciel_ht", 2800.0)) subv_80 = (b_web + b_secu) * 0.80 subv_40 = b_themis * 0.40 subv_totale = min(subv_80 + subv_40, 10000.0) rac = max((b_web + b_secu + b_themis) - subv_totale, 0.0) conn = get_db() cursor = conn.cursor() cursor.execute( """ INSERT INTO dossiers_cheque_tic ( nom_entreprise, forme_juridique, siret, code_naf, adresse_siege, commune, representant_legal, fonction_signataire, email_client, telephone_client, chiffre_affaires_actuel, douleur_actuelle, ambition_12_mois, budget_web_ht, budget_securite_ht, budget_themis_logiciel_ht, subvention_calculee, reste_a_charge_ht, statut_dossier, statut_pipeline, date_mandat, signature_electronique_validee, jalon_1_montant_ht, jalon_1_reste_charge_ht, jalon_2_montant_ht, jalon_2_reste_charge_ht, total_mission_ht ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, '6201Z', ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 65000.0, 'Gestion manuelle', 'Modernisation', ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'Mandat Signé (Web)', 'MANDAT_JALON1_SIGNE', ?, 1, 500.0, 100.0, 500.0, 100.0, 1000.0) ON CONFLICT(siret) DO UPDATE SET nom_entreprise=excluded.nom_entreprise, email_client=excluded.email_client, telephone_client=excluded.telephone_client """, ( nom_entreprise, data.get("forme_juridique", "SARL"), siret, data.get("adresse", "Non renseignée"), commune, representant, data.get("fonction", "Gérant"), email, tel, b_web, b_secu, b_themis, "application/json") self.end_headers() self.wfile.write(json.dumps({"error": str(e)}).encode("utf-8")) def log_message(self, format, *args): pass def demarrer_passerelle_themis(): try: server = HTTPServer(("0.0.0.0", PASSERELLE_PORT), MandatWebhookHandler) thread = threading.Thread(target=server.serve_forever, daemon=True) thread.start() except Exception as ex: print(f"⚠️ Erreur démarrage passerelle : {ex}") # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- # VEILLE EN ARRIÈRE-PLAN DES SIGNATURES (CORRECTION CRITIQUE 3 : client_societe) # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- def demarrer_veille_signatures_automatique(page: ft.Page): URL_GOOGLE_SHEETS_CSV = "https://docs.google.com/spreadsheets/d/e/2PACX-1vRl2qDcWIVyAftkGNT4BXef6QMfTSjc9AwHrnCfJ8695x7NzF4Kf7oBALR38R_sqOoCn43GFqqHy5G5/pub?gid=1964888021&single=true&output=tsv" def boucle_surveillance(): while True: try: # 1. Lecture avec paramètre anti-cache (Google Sheets temps réel) url_fraiche = f"{URL_GOOGLE_SHEETS_CSV}&_={int(time.time())}" df_online = pd.read_csv(url_fraiche, sep=None, engine='python', on_bad_lines='skip') if not df_online.empty: conn = None nouveaux_devis_signes = [] try: conn = get_db() conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row cursor = conn.cursor() for _, row in df_online.iterrows(): num_devis = str(row.get("Numero_Devis", "")).strip() statut_sheet = str(row.get("Statut (Valeur : EN_ATTENTE ou SIGNE)", "")).strip() date_sig = str(row.get("Date_Signatu", str(datetime.date.today()))).strip() signature_img = str(row.get("Signature_PNG", "")).strip() if num_devis and num_devis != "nan" and "SIGNE" in statut_sheet: cursor.execute("SELECT contrat_signe FROM devis WHERE numero_devis = ?", (num_devis,)) res = cursor.fetchone() # Se déclenche uniquement si le devis n'a pas encore été validé if res and res[0] == 0: cursor.execute(""" UPDATE devis SET statut = 'Validé & Signé', contrat_signe = 1, date_signature_contrat = ?, signature_b64 = ? WHERE numero_devis = ? """, (date_sig, signature_img, num_devis)) nouveaux_devis_signes.append(num_devis) conn.commit() finally: if conn: conn.close() # ⚡ DÉCLENCHEMENT DU FLUX POST-SIGNATURE UNIFIÉ (HORS CONNEXION SQLITE) if nouveaux_devis_signes: for devis_num in nouveaux_devis_signes: finaliser_signature_et_declencher_flux(devis_num, page) # Notification sobre à l'écran de Themis def notifier_ui(): devis_txt = ", ".join(nouveaux_devis_signes) show_toast(page, f"🔔 Nouveau contrat validé pour {devis_txt} !") if page.route in ["/dashboard", "/dossier", "/facturation_print", "", "/"]: page.go(page.route) page.run_thread(notifier_ui) except Exception as e: print(f"❌ [AUTO-SYNC] Erreur : {e}") time.sleep(15) # =========================================================================== # PHASE B : ENGINS FISCAUX DOM, ARCHIVAGE DÉCENNAL & ATTESTATION DE REVENUS # =========================================================================== def calculer_statut_tva_guadeloupe(ca_encaisse: float) -> dict: """ Calcule la situation fiscale exacte en Guadeloupe (TVA DOM 8,5%). Seuil franchise : 36 800 € | Seuil majoré : 39 100 €. """ SEUIL_BASE = 36800.00 SEUIL_MAJORE = 39100.00 pct_base = min(ca_encaisse / SEUIL_BASE, 1.0) pct_majore = min(ca_encaisse / SEUIL_MAJORE, 1.0) reste_base = max(SEUIL_BASE - ca_encaisse, 0.0) reste_majore = max(SEUIL_MAJORE - ca_encaisse, 0.0) if ca_encaisse < 32000.00: statut = "FRANCHISE_SEREINE" titre = "Franchise en Base de TVA (Confortable)" description = f"Vous êtes en franchise totale (Art. 293 B CGI). Reste {reste_base:,.2f} € avant le seuil de base." couleur = "#047857" bg = "#ECFDF5" elif ca_encaisse < SEUIL_BASE: statut = "VIGILANCE_SIE" titre = "Zone d'Anticipation SIE (Franchise proche)" description = f"Attention : Reste {reste_base:,.2f} € avant 36 800 €. Préparez votre demande de N° TVA au SIE de Basse-Terre / Pointe-à-Pitre." couleur = "#B45309" bg = "#FFFBEB" elif ca_encaisse < SEUIL_MAJORE: statut = "TOLERANCE_MAJOREE" titre = "Seuil de Base Dépassé — Période de Tolérance" description = f"Seuil 36 800 € franchi. Vous restez en franchise sous réserve de ne pas dépasser 39 100 € (Reste {reste_majore:,.2f} €)." couleur = "#D97706" bg = "#FEF3C7" else: statut = "ASSUJETTISSEMENT_IMMEDIAT" titre = "Seuil Majoré Dépassé (39 100 €) — TVA 8,5% OBLIGATOIRE" description = "Obligation légale de facturer la TVA à 8,5 % dès le 1er jour du mois de dépassement. Activez l'assujettissement dans Paramètres !" couleur = "#DC2626" bg = "#FEF2F2" return { "statut": statut, "titre": titre, "description": description, "couleur": couleur, "bg": bg, "pct_base": pct_base, "pct_majore": pct_majore, "reste_base": reste_base, "reste_majore": reste_majore, "seuil_base": SEUIL_BASE, "seuil_majore": SEUIL_MAJORE } def generer_attestation_revenus_12m_html(ent: dict, ca_12m: float, cotis_12m: float, ir_12m: float) -> str: """Génère l'Attestation A4 Officielle de Chiffre d'Affaires sur 12 mois glissants.""" today_fr = datetime.date.today().strftime("%d/%m/%Y") sig_b64 = ent.get("signature_prestataire_b64", "") sig_img_html = f'' if sig_b64 else '' return f""" Attestation_CA_12_Mois_{datetime.date.today().strftime('%Y%m%d')}
{ent['nom']}
{ent['adresse']}
SIRET : {ent['siret']} | Code APE : 6201Z
Micro-Entreprise BNC — Régime Spécifique Guadeloupe 		Fait en Guadeloupe, le {today_fr}
Réf Document : ATT-CA-{datetime.date.today().strftime('%Y%m%d')}

ATTESTATION DE RÉGULARITÉ & DE CHIFFRE D'AFFAIRES ENCAISSÉ

Je soussigné, {ent.get('titulaire_compte') or ent.get('nom')}, agissant en qualité de dirigeant de l'entreprise {ent['nom']} (SIRET : {ent['siret']}), certifie sur l'honneur l'exactitude des écritures comptables suivantes, extraites du Livre des Recettes officiel de l'établissement :

Période de référence (12 derniers mois glissants) Du {(datetime.date.today() - datetime.timedelta(days=365)).strftime('%d/%m/%Y')} au {today_fr}
Chiffre d'Affaires Brut Réellement Encaissé (BNC) {ca_12m:,.2f} € HT
Cotisations Sociales URSSAF ({ent.get('taux_urssaf', 21.2):.1f}%) {cotis_12m:,.2f} €
Versement Libératoire de l'Impôt sur le Revenu ({ent.get('taux_ir', 2.2):.1f}%) {ir_12m:,.2f} €
Revenu Net Professionnel de Sécurité : {(ca_12m - cotis_12m - ir_12m):,.2f} €

La présente attestation est établie en stricte conformité avec les règles du régime micro-entrepreneur (Art. 50-0 du CGI) et les décrets d'application relatifs aux départements d'outre-mer. Elle est délivrée à l'intéressé pour faire valoir ce que de droit auprès des établissements financiers, de l'ASP ou des administrations régionales.

Pour l'Entreprise {ent['nom']}
Signature manuscrite et scellement électronique eIDAS
{sig_img_html}

Document certifié conforme issu du logiciel THEMIS ERP — Enregistré dans le registre inaltérable des pièces fiscales sous empreinte SHA-256.

""" def creer_coffre_fort_decennal_zip(annee_fiscale: int, page: ft.Page) -> str: """ Génère l'archive décennale ZIP inaltérable scellée par empreinte SHA-256 conforme à l'article L102 B du Livre des Procédures Fiscales. """ today_str = datetime.date.today().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S") target_dir = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, "archives_fiscales_10_ans") os.makedirs(target_dir, exist_ok=True) zip_filename = f"ARCHIVE_FISCALE_DECENNALE_{annee_fiscale}_{today_str}.zip" zip_path = os.path.join(target_dir, zip_filename) conn = get_db() # 1. Export Livre des Recettes df_recettes = pd.read_sql_query( "SELECT * FROM paiements WHERE date_paiement LIKE ? ORDER BY date_paiement ASC", conn, params=(f"{annee_fiscale}%",) ) # 2. Export Registre des Dépenses df_depenses = pd.read_sql_query( "SELECT * FROM depenses WHERE date_depense LIKE ? ORDER BY date_depense ASC", conn, params=(f"{annee_fiscale}%",) ) # 3. Export Factures de l'exercice df_factures = pd.read_sql_query( "SELECT * FROM factures WHERE date_facture LIKE ? ORDER BY id ASC", conn, params=(f"{annee_fiscale}%",) ) conn.close() manifeste = { "norme": "L102_B_LPF_ARCHIVAGE_DECENNAL", "editeur": "THEMIS ERP ULTIMATE - Cabinet Dhamusoft", "exercice_fiscal": annee_fiscale, "date_scellement": str(datetime.datetime.now()), "fichiers": [] } try: with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_path, "w", zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zf: # Export CSV Recettes csv_rec = df_recettes.to_csv(sep=";", index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig") h_rec = hashlib.sha256(csv_rec.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest() zf.writestr(f"01_LIVRE_DES_RECETTES_{annee_fiscale}.csv", csv_rec) manifeste["fichiers"].append({"nom": f"01_LIVRE_DES_RECETTES_{annee_fiscale}.csv", "sha256": h_rec}) # Export CSV Dépenses csv_dep = df_depenses.to_csv(sep=";", index=False, encoding="utf-8-sig") h_dep = hashlib.sha256(csv_dep.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest() zf.writestr(f"02_REGISTRE_DES_DEPENSES_{annee_fiscale}.csv", csv_dep) manifeste["fichiers"].append({"nom": f"02_REGISTRE_DES_DEPENSES_{annee_fiscale}.csv", "sha256": h_dep}) # Export XML Factur-X pour chaque facture de l'exercice ent = get_entreprise_info() for _, f_row in df_factures.iterrows(): try: xml_facturx = generate_facturx_xml(dict(f_row), ent) h_xml = hashlib.sha256(xml_facturx.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest() zf.writestr(f"factures_facturx/FACTURX_{f_row['numero_facture']}.xml", xml_facturx) manifeste["fichiers"].append({"nom": f"factures_facturx/FACTURX_{f_row['numero_facture']}.xml", "sha256": h_xml}) except Exception: pass # Raccordement des scans signés du répertoire if os.path.exists(SCANS_DIR): for s_file in os.listdir(SCANS_DIR): s_path = os.path.join(SCANS_DIR, s_file) if os.path.isfile(s_path): with open(s_path, "rb") as sf: s_data = sf.read() h_s = hashlib.sha256(s_data).hexdigest() zf.writestr(f"contrats_scelles/{s_file}", s_data) manifeste["fichiers"].append({"nom": f"contrats_scelles/{s_file}", "sha256": h_s}) # Écriture du manifeste de scellement manifeste_json = json.dumps(manifeste, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) h_global = hashlib.sha256(manifeste_json.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest() manifeste["empreinte_globale_sha256"] = h_global zf.writestr("MANIFESTE_SCELLEMENT_FISCAL_SHA256.json", json.dumps(manifeste, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)) # Enregistrement en base de l'exercice clôturé tot_enc = float(df_recettes["montant_recu"].sum()) if not df_recettes.empty else 0.0 tot_fac = float(df_factures["montant_ht"].sum()) if not df_factures.empty else 0.0 conn_cl = get_db() conn_cl.cursor().execute( """ INSERT OR REPLACE INTO exercices_fiscaux (annee, date_cloture, total_encaisse, total_facture, sha256_archive) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?) """, (annee_fiscale, str(datetime.date.today()), tot_enc, tot_fac, h_global) ) conn_cl.commit() conn_cl.close() show_toast(page, f"Coffre-fort {annee_fiscale} scellé (SHA-256: {h_global[:10]}...) !") webbrowser.open(f"file://{target_dir}") return zip_path except Exception as ex: show_toast(page, f"Erreur archivage décennal : {ex}", is_error=True) return "" # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- # VUE : FISCALITÉ DOM & TÉLÉDÉCLARATION URSSAF 1-CLIC # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- def view_fiscalite_urssaf(page: ft.Page): ent = get_entreprise_info() current_year = datetime.date.today().year today = datetime.date.today() conn = get_db() df_p = pd.read_sql_query("SELECT * FROM paiements ORDER BY date_paiement ASC", conn) conn.close() taux_u = float(ent.get("taux_urssaf", 21.2)) taux_ir = float(ent.get("taux_ir", 2.2)) # Chiffre d'affaires de l'année en cours if not df_p.empty: df_an = df_p[df_p["date_paiement"].str.startswith(str(current_year))] ca_annuel = float(df_an["montant_recu"].sum()) if not df_an.empty else 0.0 else: ca_annuel = 0.0 # 1. RADAR TVA GUADELOUPE (8,5 %) tva_info = calculer_statut_tva_guadeloupe(ca_annuel) card_radar_tva_dom = create_card( ft.Column([ ft.Row([ ft.Container( content=ft.Icon(ft.icons.SHIELD_ROUNDED, color=tva_info["couleur"], size=22), bgcolor=tva_info["bg"], padding=8, border_radius=8, ), ft.Column([ ft.Text("RADAR FISCAL TVA DOM (GUADELOUPE — 8,5 %)", size=12, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_900, color="#05231A"), ft.Text(tva_info["titre"], size=13.5, weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD, color=tva_info["couleur"]), ], spacing=2, expand=True), ft.Text(f"{ca_annuel:,.2f} € HT", size=22, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_900, color="#090D16"), ], alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN), ft.Divider(height=12, color="#E2E8F0"), ft.Text(tva_info["description"], size=11.5, color="#334155"), ft.Row([ ft.Column([ ft.Row([ ft.Text("Seuil de Base (36 800 €)", size=10, weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD, color="#64748B"), ft.Text(f"{tva_info['pct_base']*100:.1f} %", size=10, weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD, color=tva_info["couleur"]), ], alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN), ft.ProgressBar(value=tva_info["pct_base"], color=tva_info["couleur"], bgcolor="#E2E8F0", height=7), ], expand=True), ft.Column([ ft.Row([ ft.Text("Seuil Majoré (39 100 €)", size=10, weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD, color="#64748B"), ft.Text(f"{tva_info['pct_majore']*100:.1f} %", size=10, weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD, color=tva_info["couleur"]), ], alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN), ft.ProgressBar(value=tva_info["pct_majore"], color=tva_info["couleur"], bgcolor="#E2E8F0", height=7), ], expand=True), ], spacing=20), ], spacing=10), padding=18 ) # 2. SÉLECTION DU TRIMESTRE & CALCULS MIROIR URSSAF quarters = { "T1": {"label": "T1 (Janvier — Mars)", "months": ["-01-", "-02-", "-03-"], "echeance": f"{current_year}-04-30", "nom": "30 Avril"}, "T2": {"label": "T2 (Avril — Juin)", "months": ["-04-", "-05-", "-06-"], "echeance": f"{current_year}-07-31", "nom": "31 Juillet"}, "T3": {"label": "T3 (Juillet — Septembre)", "months": ["-07-", "-08-", "-09-"], "echeance": f"{current_year}-10-31", "nom": "31 Octobre"}, "T4": {"label": "T4 (Octobre — Décembre)", "months": ["-10-", "-11-", "-12-"], "echeance": f"{current_year+1}-01-31", "nom": "31 Janvier"}, } trimestre_selectionne = "T1" m = today.month if m in [4, 5, 6]: trimestre_selectionne = "T2" elif m in [7, 8, 9]: trimestre_selectionne = "T3" elif m in [10, 11, 12]: trimestre_selectionne = "T4" lbl_case1_ca = ft.Text("0,00 €", size=24, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_900, color="#05231A") lbl_case2_cotis = ft.Text("0,00 €", size=20, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_900, color="#1E40AF") lbl_case3_ir = ft.Text("0,00 €", size=20, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_900, color="#B45309") lbl_total_du = ft.Text("0,00 €", size=22, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_900, color="#047857") lbl_compte_rebours = ft.Text("", size=11.5, weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD) raw_values = {"ca": 0.0, "cotis": 0.0, "ir": 0.0} def actualiser_trimestre(q_code): nonlocal trimestre_selectionne trimestre_selectionne = q_code q_info = quarters[q_code] ca_trim = 0.0 if not df_p.empty: for mo in q_info["months"]: sub = df_p[df_p["date_paiement"].str.contains(f"{current_year}{mo}")] if not sub.empty: ca_trim += sub["montant_recu"].sum() cotis = ca_trim * (taux_u / 100.0) ir_val = ca_trim * (taux_ir / 100.0) tot = cotis + ir_val raw_values["ca"] = ca_trim raw_values["cotis"] = cotis raw_values["ir"] = ir_val lbl_case1_ca.value = f"{ca_trim:,.2f} €" lbl_case2_cotis.value = f"{cotis:,.2f} €" lbl_case3_ir.value = f"{ir_val:,.2f} €" lbl_total_du.value = f"{tot:,.2f} €" d_ech = datetime.datetime.strptime(q_info["echeance"], "%Y-%m-%d").date() jours_restants = (d_ech - today).days if jours_restants > 0: lbl_compte_rebours.value = f"⏳ Échéance légale : {q_info['nom']} (Reste {jours_restants} jour(s))" lbl_compte_rebours.color = "#047857" if jours_restants > 15 else "#DC2626" else: lbl_compte_rebours.value = f"⚠️ Échéance passée ({q_info['nom']})" lbl_compte_rebours.color = "#DC2626" for code, btn in btns_trimestres.items(): btn.bgcolor = "#05231A" if code == q_code else "#F1F5F9" btn.color = ft.colors.WHITE if code == q_code else "#475569" page.update() def copier_valeur(valeur: float, label: str): texte_arrondi = f"{round(valeur)}" # L'URSSAF exige des montants entiers arrondis à l'euro le plus proche page.set_clipboard(texte_arrondi) show_toast(page, f"📋 {label} ({texte_arrondi} €) copié dans le presse-papier !") btns_trimestres = { "T1": ft.ElevatedButton("Trimestre 1 (T1)", on_click=lambda e: actualiser_trimestre("T1"), height=36), "T2": ft.ElevatedButton("Trimestre 2 (T2)", on_click=lambda e: actualiser_trimestre("T2"), height=36), "T3": ft.ElevatedButton("Trimestre 3 (T3)", on_click=lambda e: actualiser_trimestre("T3"), height=36), "T4": ft.ElevatedButton("Trimestre 4 (T4)", on_click=lambda e: actualiser_trimestre("T4"), height=36), } # Case 1 Miroir : CA Prestations BNC case1_box = ft.Container( content=ft.Column([ ft.Row([ ft.Text("CASE 1 : CHIFFRE D'AFFAIRES BNC ENCAISSÉ", size=10.5, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_800, color="#475569"), ft.ElevatedButton("Copier (1 Clic)", icon=ft.icons.COPY_ALL_ROUNDED, bgcolor="#05231A", color=ft.colors.WHITE, height=28, on_click=lambda e: copier_valeur(raw_values["ca"], "CA BNC")), ], alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN), lbl_case1_ca, ft.Text("À coller dans 'Prestations de services BNC' sur autoentrepreneur.urssaf.fr", size=10, color="#64748B"), ], spacing=4), bgcolor="#F8FAFC", border=ft.border.all(1.5, "#CBD5E1"), border_radius=8, padding=14, expand=True ) # Case 2 Miroir : Cotisations URSSAF case2_box = ft.Container( content=ft.Column([ ft.Row([ ft.Text(f"CASE 2 : COTISATIONS SOCIALES ({taux_u} %)", size=10.5, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_800, color="#1E40AF"), ft.ElevatedButton("Copier", icon=ft.icons.COPY_ALL_ROUNDED, bgcolor="#1E40AF", color=ft.colors.WHITE, height=28, on_click=lambda e: copier_valeur(raw_values["cotis"], "Cotisations")), ], alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN), lbl_case2_cotis, ft.Text("Montant exact déduit automatiquement par le portail", size=10, color="#64748B"), ], spacing=4), bgcolor="#F8FAFC", border=ft.border.all(1.5, "#CBD5E1"), border_radius=8, padding=14, expand=True ) # Case 3 Miroir : Versement Libératoire IR case3_box = ft.Container( content=ft.Column([ ft.Row([ ft.Text(f"CASE 3 : PRÉLÈVEMENT LIBÉRATOIRE IR ({taux_ir} %)", size=10.5, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_800, color="#B45309"), ft.ElevatedButton("Copier", icon=ft.icons.COPY_ALL_ROUNDED, bgcolor="#B45309", color=ft.colors.WHITE, height=28, on_click=lambda e: copier_valeur(raw_values["ir"], "Impôt IR")), ], alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN), lbl_case3_ir, ft.Text("Option fiscale libératoire de l'impôt sur le revenu", size=10, color="#64748B"), ], spacing=4), bgcolor="#F8FAFC", border=ft.border.all(1.5, "#CBD5E1"), border_radius=8, padding=14, expand=True ) card_bordereau = create_card( ft.Column([ ft.Row([ ft.Icon(ft.icons.CONTENT_PASTE_ROUNDED, color="#05231A", size=20), ft.Text(f"BORDEREAU DÉCLARATIF TRIMESTRIEL URSSAF EN 1 CLIC — EXERCICE {current_year}", size=13, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_900, color="#05231A"), ], spacing=6), ft.Row(list(btns_trimestres.values()), spacing=8), lbl_compte_rebours, ft.Divider(height=10, color="#E2E8F0"), ft.Row([case1_box, case2_box, case3_box], spacing=12), ft.Container( content=ft.Row([ ft.Column([ ft.Text("TOTAL GLOBAL À TÉLÉPAYER À L'ÉCHÉANCE :", size=11, weight=ft.FontWeight.BOLD, color="#065F46"), lbl_total_du, ], spacing=2), ft.ElevatedButton( "🌐 Ouvrir le Portail autoentrepreneur.urssaf.fr", icon=ft.icons.OPEN_IN_NEW_ROUNDED, bgcolor="#05231A", color=ft.colors.WHITE, height=42, on_click=lambda e: webbrowser.open("https://www.autoentrepreneur.urssaf.fr") ), ], alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN), bgcolor="#ECFDF5", border=ft.border.all(1, "#A7F3D0"), border_radius=8, padding=14 ), ], spacing=12), padding=18 ) # 3. ACTIONS RAPIDES : ATTESTATION 12 MOIS & COFFRE-FORT DÉCENNAL def imprimer_attestation(e): # Calcul des 12 derniers mois glissants limite_12m = (datetime.date.today() - datetime.timedelta(days=365)).strftime("%Y-%m-%d") conn_a = get_db() df_12 = pd.read_sql_query("SELECT SUM(montant_recu) FROM paiements WHERE date_paiement >= ?", conn_a, params=(limite_12m,)) ca_12 = float(df_12.iloc[0][0] or 0.0) conn_a.close() cotis_12 = ca_12 * (taux_u / 100.0) ir_12 = ca_12 * (taux_ir / 100.0) html = generer_attestation_revenus_12m_html(ent, ca_12, cotis_12, ir_12) with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile("w", delete=False, suffix=".html", encoding="utf-8") as tf: tf.write(html) temp_path = tf.name webbrowser.open(f"file://{temp_path}") show_toast(page, "Attestation officielle sur 12 mois ouverte !") card_outils_fiscaux = create_card( ft.Row([ ft.Column([ ft.Text("Attestation de Revenus Officielle (12 Mois Glissants)", size=13.5, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_800, color="#05231A"), ft.Text("Document A4 scellé pour les banques, le Chèque TIC et les dossiers ASP.", size=11, color="#64748B"), ], expand=True), ft.Row([ ft.ElevatedButton( "📄 Éditer l'Attestation A4", icon=ft.icons.PRINT_ROUNDED, bgcolor="#047857", color=ft.colors.WHITE, height=42, on_click=imprimer_attestation ), ft.ElevatedButton( f"🔒 Sceller Coffre-Fort {current_year} (ZIP L102 B)", icon=ft.icons.ARCHIVE_ROUNDED, bgcolor="#05231A", color=ft.colors.WHITE, height=42, on_click=lambda e: creer_coffre_fort_decennal_zip(current_year, page) ), ], spacing=10), ], alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN), padding=18 ) actualiser_trimestre(trimestre_selectionne) return ft.ListView( controls=[ create_header("FIS.01", "FISCALITÉ DOM, BORDEREAU URSSAF & COFFRE-FORT", "DÉCLARATION EN 1 CLIC • RADAR TVA GUADELOUPE (8,5%) & SÉCURITÉ FISCALE 10 ANS"), card_radar_tva_dom, ft.Container(height=12), card_bordereau, ft.Container(height=12), card_outils_fiscaux, build_footer(), ], spacing=10, expand=True, ) # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- # POINT D'ENTRÉE PRINCIPAL & NAVIGATION FLET # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- def main(page: ft.Page): init_db() demarrer_passerelle_themis() demarrer_veille_signatures_automatique(page) page.title = "Themis ERP - Micro-Entreprise & B2B" page.bgcolor = THEME["bg"] page.padding = 0 page.fonts = { "Manrope": "https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Manrope:wght@400;500;600;700;800;900&display=swap" } page.theme = ft.Theme(font_family="Manrope") try: if os.path.exists(ICO_PATH): page.window.icon = ICO_PATH except Exception: pass content_area = ft.Container(expand=True, padding=ft.padding.symmetric(horizontal=28, vertical=20)) def route_change(e): route = page.route if route in ["/dashboard", "", "/"]: content_area.content = view_dashboard(page) elif route == "/guide": content_area.content = view_livret_accueil(page) elif route == "/kanban": content_area.content = view_kanban(page) elif route == "/mco": content_area.content = view_mco_cockpit(page) elif route == "/cheque_tic": content_area.content = view_cheque_tic(page) elif route == "/crm": content_area.content = view_crm_clients(page) elif route == "/catalogue": content_area.content = view_catalogue(page) elif route == "/devis": content_area.content = view_devis_form(page) elif route == "/archives": content_area.content = view_archives(page) elif route == "/dossier": content_area.content = view_dossier_contractuel(page) elif route == "/factures": content_area.content = view_factures(page) elif route == "/facturation_print": content_area.content = view_facture_interactive(page) elif route == "/livre_recettes": content_area.content = view_livre_recettes(page) elif route == "/depenses": content_area.content = view_depenses(page) elif route == "/fiscalite": content_area.content = view_fiscalite_urssaf(page) elif route == "/settings": content_area.content = view_settings(page) update_sidebar() page.update() page.on_route_change = route_change sidebar_expanded = True sidebar_container = ft.Container( width=260, bgcolor="#FFFFFF", border=ft.border.only(right=ft.BorderSide(1, "#CBD5E1")), padding=ft.padding.symmetric(horizontal=12, vertical=16), animate=ft.animation.Animation(250, "decelerate"), ) def toggle_sidebar(e): nonlocal sidebar_expanded sidebar_expanded = not sidebar_expanded update_sidebar() def nav_sub_btn(icon, text, route): is_active = (page.route == route) def click(e): page.go(route) active_border = ft.border.only(left=ft.BorderSide(3.5, "#047857")) if (is_active and sidebar_expanded) else None bg_color = "#ECFDF5" if is_active else ft.colors.TRANSPARENT icon_color = "#047857" if is_active else "#64748B" text_color = "#090D16" if is_active else "#475569" return ft.Container( content=ft.Row([ ft.Icon(icon, size=18, color=icon_color), ft.Text(text, size=12, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_800 if is_active else ft.FontWeight.W_600, color=text_color, visible=sidebar_expanded), ], spacing=12, alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.START if sidebar_expanded else ft.MainAxisAlignment.CENTER), padding=ft.padding.symmetric(horizontal=12, vertical=10) if sidebar_expanded else ft.padding.all(10), border_radius=ft.border_radius.only(top_right=8, bottom_right=8) if sidebar_expanded else 8, border=active_border, bgcolor=bg_color, on_click=click, ink=True, tooltip=text if not sidebar_expanded else None ) def section_title(text): return ft.Container( content=ft.Text(text, size=10, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_800, color="#94A3B8"), padding=ft.padding.only(left=12, top=16, bottom=4), visible=sidebar_expanded ) def update_sidebar(): sidebar_container.width = 260 if sidebar_expanded else 72 sidebar_container.padding = ft.padding.symmetric(horizontal=12, vertical=16) if sidebar_expanded else ft.padding.symmetric(horizontal=8, vertical=16) b64_logo = get_app_logo_base64() logo_widget = ft.Container( content=ft.Image(src_base64=b64_logo, width=40 if sidebar_expanded else 32, height=40 if sidebar_expanded else 32, fit=ft.ImageFit.CONTAIN, border_radius=8) if b64_logo else ft.Icon(ft.icons.BALANCE_ROUNDED, color="#047857", size=28), alignment=ft.alignment.center, ) header_content = ft.Row([ logo_widget, ft.Column([ ft.Text("THEMIS ERP", size=16, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_900, color="#090D16"), ft.Text("SUITE COMMERCIALE", size=8, weight=ft.FontWeight.W_800, color="#047857"), ], spacing=0, visible=sidebar_expanded), ], spacing=12, alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.START if sidebar_expanded else ft.MainAxisAlignment.CENTER) toggle_btn = ft.IconButton( icon=ft.icons.MENU_OPEN_ROUNDED if sidebar_expanded else ft.icons.MENU_ROUNDED, icon_color="#94A3B8", icon_size=20, on_click=toggle_sidebar, tooltip="Réduire le menu" if sidebar_expanded else "Développer le menu", style=ft.ButtonStyle(shape=ft.RoundedRectangleBorder(radius=6)) ) top_bar = ft.Row([header_content, toggle_btn], alignment=ft.MainAxisAlignment.SPACE_BETWEEN) if not sidebar_expanded: top_bar = ft.Column([header_content, toggle_btn], horizontal_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER, spacing=15) nav_items = [ top_bar, ft.Divider(color="#E2E8F0", height=20), section_title("GÉNÉRAL"), nav_sub_btn(ft.icons.DASHBOARD_ROUNDED, "Tableau de Bord", "/dashboard"), nav_sub_btn(ft.icons.MENU_BOOK_ROUNDED, "Manuel d'Exploitation", "/guide"), section_title("PRODUCTION"), nav_sub_btn(ft.icons.VIEW_KANBAN_ROUNDED, "Projets & Kanban (4P)", "/kanban"), nav_sub_btn(ft.icons.HEALTH_AND_SAFETY_ROUNDED, "Cockpit MCO", "/mco"), section_title("COMMERCIAL"), nav_sub_btn(ft.icons.ASSIGNMENT_ROUNDED, "Dossiers Chèque TIC", "/cheque_tic"), nav_sub_btn(ft.icons.PEOPLE_ALT_ROUNDED, "Portefeuille & 360°", "/crm"), nav_sub_btn(ft.icons.INVENTORY_2_ROUNDED, "Catalogue Prestations", "/catalogue"), nav_sub_btn(ft.icons.DESCRIPTION_ROUNDED, "Devis & Contrats", "/devis"), nav_sub_btn(ft.icons.ARCHIVE_ROUNDED, "Archives", "/archives"), section_title("FACTURATION"), nav_sub_btn(ft.icons.GAVEL_ROUNDED, "Dossier Légal (7P)", "/dossier"), nav_sub_btn(ft.icons.POST_ADD_ROUNDED, "Émettre Facture", "/factures"), nav_sub_btn(ft.icons.RECEIPT_LONG_ROUNDED, "Aperçu & Factur-X", "/facturation_print"), section_title("FINANCE"), nav_sub_btn(ft.icons.ACCOUNT_BALANCE_ROUNDED, "Bordereau URSSAF & TVA", "/fiscalite"), nav_sub_btn(ft.icons.SAVINGS_ROUNDED, "Livre des Recettes", "/livre_recettes"), nav_sub_btn(ft.icons.SHOPPING_BAG_ROUNDED, "Registre Dépenses", "/depenses"), section_title("SYSTÈME"), nav_sub_btn(ft.icons.SETTINGS_ROUNDED, "Paramètres & Fiscalité", "/settings"), ] sidebar_container.content = ft.Column( controls=nav_items, spacing=2, scroll=ft.ScrollMode.AUTO, horizontal_alignment=ft.CrossAxisAlignment.START if sidebar_expanded else ft.CrossAxisAlignment.CENTER ) if sidebar_container.page: sidebar_container.update() def build_sidebar(): update_sidebar() return sidebar_container page.add(ft.Row(controls=[build_sidebar(), content_area], expand=True, spacing=0)) page.go("/dashboard") # =========================================================================== # POINT DE DÉMARRAGE DE L'APPLICATION # =========================================================================== if __name__ == "__main__": ft.app(target=main, view=ft.AppView.WEB_BROWSER)
